### Tool call: browser_evaluate
- Args
```json
{
  "function": "async () => {\n   const expected=null,deadline=Date.now()+3000;\n   while(expected!==null && vm.runtime.getTargetForStage().variables.var_steps.value!==expected && Date.now()<deadline) await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,16));\n   const t=vm.runtime.targets.find(t=>t.getName()==='Harness Robot');\n   return {x:t.x,y:t.y,variables:Object.fromEntries(Object.values(vm.runtime.getTargetForStage().variables).map(v=>[v.name,v.value])),threads:vm.runtime.threads.length,errors:window.__nativeErrors};\n }"
}
```
- Result
```json
{
  "result": "{\n  \"x\": 10,\n  \"y\": 0,\n  \"variables\": {\n    \"steps\": 5,\n    \"clicks\": 2,\n    \"ready\": 1\n  },\n  \"threads\": 1,\n  \"errors\": []\n}",
  "code": "await page.evaluate('async () => {\\n   const expected=null,deadline=Date.now()+3000;\\n   while(expected!==null && vm.runtime.getTargetForStage().variables.var_steps.value!==expected && Date.now()<deadline) await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,16));\\n   const t=vm.runtime.targets.find(t=>t.getName()===\\'Harness Robot\\');\\n   return {x:t.x,y:t.y,variables:Object.fromEntries(Object.values(vm.runtime.getTargetForStage().variables).map(v=>[v.name,v.value])),threads:vm.runtime.threads.length,errors:window.__nativeErrors};\\n }');"
}
```

### Tool call: browser_evaluate
- Args
```json
{
  "function": "async () => {\n  const deadline=Date.now()+3000;while(vm.runtime.getTargetForStage().variables.var_steps.value!==1&&Date.now()<deadline)await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,16));\n  const t=vm.runtime.targets.find(t=>t.getName()==='Harness Robot'),v=vm.runtime.getTargetForStage().variables;\n  if(t.x!==10||t.y!==0||v.var_steps.value!==1||v.var_ready.value!==1)throw Error('Reload regression failed');\n  if(window.__nativeErrors.length)throw Error('Page or compilation errors found');\n  return {x:t.x,y:t.y,steps:v.var_steps.value,ready:v.var_ready.value,errors:window.__nativeErrors};\n}"
}
```

{
  "function": "async () => {\n const deadline=Date.now()+3000;\n while(vm.runtime.getTargetForStage().variables.v_忙碌.value!==0&&Date.now()<deadline)await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,16));\n const v=vm.runtime.getTargetForStage().variables;\n const unchanged=window.__nnModelBeforeTest===JSON.stringify(['l_W1','l_B1','l_W2','v_B2','l_样本X','l_样本Y','v_训练轮次'].map(id=>v[id].value));\n if(Math.abs(v.v_预测输出.value-3)>.35||!unchanged)throw Error('Native prediction or immutability failed');\n return {status:'PASS',shortcut:'3',input:v.v_测试输入.value,prediction:v.v_预测输出.value,modelUnchanged:unchanged};\n}"
}

### Tool call: browser_evaluate
- Args
```json
{
  "function": "async () => {\n const end=Date.now()+15000;while(vm.runtime.getTargetForStage().variables.v_忙碌.value!==0&&Date.now()<end)await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,16));\n const v=vm.runtime.getTargetForStage().variables;\n if(v.v_样本数.value!==1||v.v_训练轮次.value!==600||v.l_样本X.value[0]!==-1||v.l_样本Y.value[0]!==-2)throw Error('Training failed');\n return {status:'PASS',shortcut:'2',samples:v.v_样本数.value,epochs:v.v_训练轮次.value,MSE:v.v_均方误差.value};\n}"
}
```

}

### Tool call: browser_evaluate
- Args
```json
{
  "function": "() => ({ready:!!window.vm?.runtime?.targets.some(t=>!t.isStage&&t.getCostumes().length>0), canvases:[...document.querySelectorAll('canvas')].map(c=>({width:c.width,height:c.height})), buttons:[...document.querySelectorAll('img')].map(x=>({alt:x.alt,title:x.title})).filter(x=>/flag|stage|full/i.test(x.alt+' '+x.title))})"
}
```
- Result
```json
{
  "result": "{\n  \"ready\": true,\n  \"canvases\": [\n    {\n      \"width\": 480,\n      \"height\": 360\n    },\n    {\n      \"width\": 0,\n      \"height\": 0\n    }\n  ],\n  \"buttons\": [\n    {\n      \"alt\": \"Switch to full stage\",\n      \"title\": \"Full Screen Control\"\n    }\n  ]\n}",
  "code": "await page.evaluate('() => ({ready:!!window.vm?.runtime?.targets.some(t=>!t.isStage&&t.getCostumes().length>0), canvases:[...document.querySelectorAll(\\'canvas\\')].map(c=>({width:c.width,height:c.height})), buttons:[...document.querySelectorAll(\\'img\\')].map(x=>({alt:x.alt,title:x.title})).filter(x=>/flag|stage|full/i.test(x.alt+\\' \\'+x.title))})');"
}
```
